			_ocean_level = rng.randf_range(0.46, 0.58)
			_has_clouds = true
		"arid":
			_low = Color("#5a2e1c")
			_mid = Color("#c47a4a")
			_high = Color("#e8d2a8")
			_ocean = Color("#3a4a38")
			_ice = Color("#ece4d4")
			atmo_color = Color(0.82, 0.58, 0.38, 0.14)
			_ocean_level = rng.randf_range(0.2, 0.32)
			atmo_scale = 1.14
		"ice":
			_style = 2
			_low = Color("#4a6580")
			_mid = Color("#9bb8c8")
			_high = Color("#eef4f8")
			_ocean = Color("#3a6080")
			_ice = Color("#f4f8fc")
			atmo_color = Color(0.72, 0.84, 0.94, 0.18)
			_ocean_level = 0.2
		"lava":
			_style = 3
			_low = Color("#1a1210")
			_mid = Color("#4a2218")
			_high = Color("#e07030")
			_ocean = Color("#801808")
			_ice = Color("#3a2018")
			_glow = Color(1.0, 0.32, 0.06, 0.9)
			atmo_color = Color(0.9, 0.35, 0.12, 0.2)
			_ocean_level = 0.38
		"gas":
			_style = 1
			_low = Color("#6a3a28")
			_mid = Color("#d4a05a")
			_high = Color("#f0d8a8")
			_ocean = _mid
			_ice = _high
			atmo_color = Color(0.88, 0.7, 0.42, 0.2)
			atmo_scale = 1.28
			has_rings = rng.randf() > 0.32
			if rng.randf() > 0.5:
				_low = Color("#3a4a78")
				_mid = Color("#8aa0d0")
				_high = Color("#d8e4f0")
				atmo_color = Color(0.55, 0.68, 0.88, 0.22)
		"toxic":
			_low = Color("#2a3a18")
			_mid = Color("#7a9a32")
			_high = Color("#c8d86a")
			_ocean = Color("#3a5818")
			_ice = Color("#dce8a8")
			atmo_color = Color(0.62, 0.84, 0.28, 0.2)
			_has_clouds = true
			_glow = Color(0.2, 0.35, 0.05, 0.4)
			_ocean_level = 0.4
		"carbon":
			_low = Color("#121018")
			_mid = Color("#3a2a48")
			_high = Color("#8a6aa0")
			_ocean = Color("#1a1028")
			_ice = Color("#c8b8d8")
			atmo_color = Color(0.42, 0.28, 0.55, 0.16)
			_ocean_level = 0.3
		_:
			_low = Color("#3a2a22")
			_mid = Color("#c47a4a")
			_high = Color("#e6c99a")
			_ocean = Color("#1c3a42")
			_ice = Color("#dce8ee")
	if has_rings:
		ring_inner = rng.randf_range(1.35, 1.55)
		ring_outer = rng.randf_range(2.0, 2.55)
		ring_tilt = rng.randf_range(0.18, 0.38)


func map_color() -> Color:
	return _mid


func surface_palette() -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"deep": _ocean,
		"low": _low,
		"mid": _mid,
		"high": _high,
		"ice": _ice,
		"ocean_level": _ocean_level,
	}


func is_moon() -> bool:
	return parent_body != null


func surface_from_world(world: Vector2) -> SurfaceCoordinate:
	var local := to_local(world) / maxf(radius, 1.0)
	if local.length() > 1.0:
		local = local.normalized()
	var depth := sqrt(maxf(0.0, 1.0 - local.length_squared()))
	return SurfaceCoordinate.from_unit_vector(Vector3(local.x, -local.y, depth))


func n_at(ang: float, scale: float = 3.2) -> float:
	var c := Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * scale
	return _noise.get_noise_2d(c.x + seed_value, c.y - seed_value * 0.3) * 0.5 + 0.5


func tick(delta: float) -> void:
	orbit_angle += orbit_speed * delta
	rotate(spin_speed * delta)
	_cloud_spin += spin_speed * 0.35 * delta
	var center := parent_body.global_position if parent_body else Vector2.ZERO
	global_position = center + Vector2(cos(orbit_angle), sin(orbit_angle)) * orbit_radius
	if _sphere_visual and _sphere_visual.material is ShaderMaterial:
		var sun_local := to_local(Vector2.ZERO)
		var light_direction := sun_local.normalized() if sun_local.length_squared() > 1.0 else Vector2(-0.7, -0.45)
		(_sphere_visual.material as ShaderMaterial).set_shader_parameter("light_direction", light_direction)
	queue_redraw()


func inertial_velocity() -> Vector2:
	var center_vel := Vector2.ZERO
	if parent_body is ProcPlanet:
		center_vel = (parent_body as ProcPlanet).inertial_velocity()
	var tangent := Vector2(-sin(orbit_angle), cos(orbit_angle))
	return center_vel + tangent * (orbit_speed * orbit_radius)


func gravity_at(world: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	return OrbitalPhysics.gravity_at(world, global_position, mu, radius * 0.55)


func _draw() -> void:
	_draw_atmosphere()
	if has_rings:
		_draw_rings(0.55)


func _draw_atmosphere() -> void:
	var layers := 5
	for i in layers:
		var t := float(i) / float(layers)
		var r := radius * lerpf(1.02, atmo_scale, t)
		var c := atmo_color
		c.a = atmo_color.a * (1.0 - t) * 0.85
		draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, r, c)


func _draw_rings(alpha: float) -> void:
	var xf := Transform2D(0.45, Vector2.ZERO)
	xf.y *= ring_tilt
	draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.45, Vector2(1.0, ring_tilt))
	var inner := radius * ring_inner
	var outer := radius * ring_outer
	var col := Color(0.82, 0.7, 0.5, alpha)
	if kind == "gas" and _low.b > 0.4:
		col = Color(0.7, 0.78, 0.88, alpha)
	var pts_o := PackedVector2Array()
	var pts_i := PackedVector2Array()
	var n := 96
	for i in n + 1:
		var a := TAU * float(i) / float(n)
		pts_o.append(Vector2(cos(a), sin(a)) * outer)
		pts_i.append(Vector2(cos(a), sin(a)) * inner)
	for i in n:
		var poly := PackedVector2Array([pts_i[i], pts_o[i], pts_o[i + 1], pts_i[i + 1]])
		var gap := absf(sin(float(i) * 0.55))
		var c := col
		c.a *= 0.45 + gap * 0.55
		if absf(float(i) / n - 0.62) < 0.04:
			c.a *= 0.15
		draw_colored_polygon(poly, c)
	draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2.ONE)
	xf = xf


func _draw_body() -> void:
	draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, radius, _mid)
	draw_arc(Vector2.ZERO, radius, 0.0, TAU, 96, Color(0.75, 0.9, 1.0, 0.28), 2.0, true)


func _rim(ang: float) -> Vector2:
	var bump := 0.0 if _style == 1 else (n_at(ang, 4.0) - 0.5) * 0.07
	return Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * radius * (1.0 + bump)


func _color_at(ang: float) -> Color:
	var n := n_at(ang, 3.4)
	if _style == 1:
		var bands := sin(sin(ang) * 14.0 + n * 4.0)
		var col := _low.lerp(_mid, n)
		return col.lerp(_high, clampf(bands * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0))
	if _style == 2:
		return _low.lerp(_ice, clampf(n * 1.15, 0.0, 1.0)).lerp(_high, smoothstep(0.55, 0.85, n_at(ang, 8.0)))
	var ocean := 1.0 - smoothstep(_ocean_level - 0.04, _ocean_level + 0.03, n)
	var land := _low.lerp(_mid, clampf((n - _ocean_level) * 3.0, 0.0, 1.0))
	land = land.lerp(_high, smoothstep(0.62, 0.88, n))
	var col := land.lerp(_ocean, ocean)
	var ice := smoothstep(0.72, 0.95, absf(sin(ang)))
	col = col.lerp(_ice, ice * (0.85 if kind == "ocean" or kind == "ice" else 0.35))
	return col


func _draw_clouds(sun_local: Vector2) -> void:
	var segs := 72
	for i in segs:
		var ang := TAU * float(i) / float(segs) + _cloud_spin
		var cover := n_at(ang, 5.5)
		if cover < 0.55:
			continue
		var a0 := ang
		var a1 := ang + TAU / float(segs)
		var r := radius * 1.02
		var p0 := Vector2(cos(a0), sin(a0)) * r
		var p1 := Vector2(cos(a1), sin(a1)) * r
		var lit := clampf(Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)).dot(sun_local) * 0.4 + 0.6, 0.25, 1.0)
		var c := Color(0.93, 0.9, 0.86, (cover - 0.55) * 1.3 * 0.55)
		c = Color(c.r * lit, c.g * lit, c.b * lit, c.a)
		draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([Vector2.ZERO, p0, p1]), c)



##### backup-units-20260915-175616\scripts\ship.gd #####
class_name SurveyShip
extends CharacterBody2D

const DRY_MASS := 8.0
const FUEL_MASS := 4.0
const MAIN_THRUST := 240.0
const RCS_THRUST := 28.0
const TORQUE := 1.05
const SAS_TORQUE := 1.35
const MAX_SPIN := 1.15
const HULL_R := 28.0
const MAX_SAFE_LANDING_RADIAL_SPEED := 12.0
const MAX_SAFE_LANDING_TANGENT_SPEED := 16.0
const CAMERA_FOLLOW_SPEED := 5.5
const CAMERA_ZOOM_MIN := 0.00005
const CAMERA_ZOOM_MAX := 12.0
const CAMERA_ZOOM_STEP := 1.25
const FUEL_MAX := 100.0
const HULL_MAX := 100.0
const ENERGY_MAX := 100.0

var throttle: float = 0.0
var angular_velocity: float = 0.0
var sas: bool = true
var camera_zoom: float = 0.85
var time_warp: float = 1.0
var fuel: float = FUEL_MAX
var hull: float = HULL_MAX
var energy: float = ENERGY_MAX
var universe: Universe
var spawn_point: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
var landed_on: ProcPlanet = null
var landed_angle: float = 0.0
var surface_coordinate := SurfaceCoordinate.new()
var surface_mode_active: bool = false
var autopilot_target: ProcPlanet = null
var autopilot_phase: String = "off"
var autopilot_delta_v: float = 0.0
var _autopilot_heading: float = 0.0

var _cam: Camera2D
var _aiming: bool = false


func _ready() -> void:
	motion_mode = CharacterBody2D.MOTION_MODE_FLOATING
	z_index = 8
	var shape := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = HULL_R
	shape.shape = circle
	add_child(shape)
	_cam = Camera2D.new()
	_cam.enabled = true
	_cam.top_level = true
	_cam.position_smoothing_enabled = false
	_cam.process_callback = Camera2D.CAMERA2D_PROCESS_PHYSICS
	_cam.ignore_rotation = true
	add_child(_cam)
	_cam.global_position = global_position
	_cam.make_current()
	if spawn_point != Vector2.ZERO:
		global_position = spawn_point


func _update_camera(delta: float) -> void:
	if _cam == null:
		return
	var follow := 1.0 - exp(-CAMERA_FOLLOW_SPEED * delta)
	_cam.global_position = _cam.global_position.lerp(global_position, follow)


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if surface_mode_active:
		return
	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed:
		if event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
			camera_zoom = clampf(camera_zoom * CAMERA_ZOOM_STEP, CAMERA_ZOOM_MIN, CAMERA_ZOOM_MAX)
		elif event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
			camera_zoom = clampf(camera_zoom / CAMERA_ZOOM_STEP, CAMERA_ZOOM_MIN, CAMERA_ZOOM_MAX)
	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		match event.physical_keycode:
			KEY_EQUAL, KEY_KP_ADD:
				camera_zoom = clampf(camera_zoom * CAMERA_ZOOM_STEP, CAMERA_ZOOM_MIN, CAMERA_ZOOM_MAX)
			KEY_MINUS, KEY_KP_SUBTRACT:
				camera_zoom = clampf(camera_zoom / CAMERA_ZOOM_STEP, CAMERA_ZOOM_MIN, CAMERA_ZOOM_MAX)
			KEY_0, KEY_KP_0:
				var planet := universe.nearest(global_position) if universe else null
				if planet:
					frame_near(planet)
			KEY_T:
				sas = not sas
			KEY_X:
				throttle = 0.0
			KEY_Z:
				throttle = 1.0
			KEY_PERIOD:
				_cycle_warp()


func _cycle_warp() -> void:
	if time_warp < 1.5:
		time_warp = 4.0
	elif time_warp < 8.0:
		time_warp = 16.0
	else:
		time_warp = 1.0
	if absf(throttle) > 0.04:
		time_warp = 1.0


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if surface_mode_active:
		time_warp = 1.0
		queue_redraw()
		return
	var manual_input := _has_control_input()
	if manual_input and autopilot_target != null:
		cancel_autopilot()
	if manual_input or (absf(throttle) > 0.04 and autopilot_target == null):
		time_warp = 1.0
	if autopilot_target != null:
		time_warp = 1.0
		_update_autopilot_guidance()
	else:
		_throttle_input(delta)
	if landed_on != null:
		_tick_landed(delta)
	else:
		var steps := maxi(1, int(ceil(time_warp)))
		var step := delta * time_warp / float(steps)
		for i in steps:
			if autopilot_target != null:
				_steer_to(_autopilot_heading, step)
				_update_autopilot_guidance()
			else:
				_steer(step)
			_apply_forces(step)
			_move_step(step)
			_update_energy(step)
			if landed_on != null:
				break
	_update_camera(delta)
	_cam.zoom = _cam.zoom.lerp(Vector2(camera_zoom, camera_zoom), 1.0 - exp(-6.0 * delta))
	queue_redraw()


func _has_control_input() -> bool:
	return (
		Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_W)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_S)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_UP)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_DOWN)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_A)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_D)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_Q)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_E)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_SHIFT)
		or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT)
	)


func _steer(dt: float) -> void:
	var yaw := float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_D)) - float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_A))
	_aiming = Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT)
	if _aiming:
		var want := (get_global_mouse_position() - global_position).angle()
		var err := angle_difference(rotation, want)
		var target_spin := clampf(err * 1.8, -MAX_SPIN, MAX_SPIN)
		angular_velocity = move_toward(angular_velocity, target_spin, TORQUE * 1.4 * dt)
	elif absf(yaw) > 0.01:
		angular_velocity += yaw * TORQUE * dt
	elif sas and energy > 0.0:
		angular_velocity = move_toward(angular_velocity, 0.0, SAS_TORQUE * dt)
	angular_velocity = clampf(angular_velocity, -MAX_SPIN, MAX_SPIN)
	rotation += angular_velocity * dt


func _steer_to(wanted_angle: float, dt: float) -> void:
	var error := angle_difference(rotation, wanted_angle)
	var target_spin := clampf(error * 1.8, -MAX_SPIN, MAX_SPIN)
	angular_velocity = move_toward(angular_velocity, target_spin, TORQUE * 1.6 * dt)
	angular_velocity = clampf(angular_velocity, -MAX_SPIN, MAX_SPIN)
	rotation += angular_velocity * dt


func _throttle_input(dt: float) -> void:
	if Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_W) or (landed_on == null and Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_UP)):
		var throttle_rate := 0.9 if landed_on != null else 0.55
		throttle = move_toward(throttle, 1.0, throttle_rate * dt)
	if Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_S) or (landed_on == null and Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_DOWN)):
		throttle = move_toward(throttle, -0.28, 0.55 * dt)
	if Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_SHIFT):
		throttle = move_toward(throttle, 1.0, 1.4 * dt)


func _apply_forces(dt: float) -> void:
	var forward := Vector2.from_angle(rotation)
	if fuel <= 0.0:
		throttle = 0.0
	if absf(throttle) > 0.01 and fuel > 0.0:
		var thrust := MAIN_THRUST * throttle / mass()
		velocity += forward * thrust * dt
		fuel = maxf(0.0, fuel - absf(throttle) * 1.35 * dt)
	var rcs := Vector2.ZERO
	if Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_Q):
		rcs += Vector2(-forward.y, forward.x)
	if Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_E):
		rcs -= Vector2(-forward.y, forward.x)
	if rcs.length() > 0.01 and fuel > 0.0:
		velocity += rcs.normalized() * (RCS_THRUST / mass()) * dt
		fuel = maxf(0.0, fuel - 0.45 * dt)
	if universe:
		velocity += universe.gravity_at(global_position) * dt


func mass() -> float:
	return DRY_MASS + FUEL_MASS * fuel / FUEL_MAX


func _move_step(dt: float) -> void:
	var velocity_before := velocity
	var hit := move_and_collide(velocity * dt)
	if hit == null:
		return
	var normal := hit.get_normal()
	var normal_speed := maxf(0.0, -normal.dot(velocity_before))
	var tangent_speed := absf(normal.orthogonal().dot(velocity_before))
	var collider := hit.get_collider() as Node
	var planet := collider.get_parent() as ProcPlanet if collider else null
	if (
		planet
		and normal_speed <= MAX_SAFE_LANDING_RADIAL_SPEED
		and tangent_speed <= MAX_SAFE_LANDING_TANGENT_SPEED
	):
		_land_after_contact(planet)
		return
	if normal_speed > MAX_SAFE_LANDING_RADIAL_SPEED:
		hull = maxf(0.0, hull - (normal_speed - MAX_SAFE_LANDING_RADIAL_SPEED) * 0.7)
	velocity = velocity_before.bounce(normal) * 0.28
	global_position += normal * 2.0


func _update_energy(dt: float) -> void:
	if sas and absf(angular_velocity) > 0.015:
		energy = maxf(0.0, energy - 2.4 * dt)
	else:
		energy = move_toward(energy, ENERGY_MAX, 1.2 * dt)


func _tick_landed(delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(landed_on):
		landed_on = null
		return
	landed_angle = surface_coordinate.longitude
	var world_angle := surface_coordinate.longitude + landed_on.rotation
	var outward := Vector2.from_angle(world_angle)
	var surface_radius := landed_on.radius + HULL_R + 2.0
	global_position = landed_on.global_position + outward * surface_radius
	velocity = landed_on.inertial_velocity() + outward.orthogonal() * landed_on.spin_speed * surface_radius
	rotation = world_angle
	angular_velocity = landed_on.spin_speed
	if autopilot_target != null:
		autopilot_phase = "launch"
		_autopilot_heading = world_angle
		throttle = 1.0
	_update_energy(delta)
	var thrust_acceleration := MAIN_THRUST * maxf(throttle, 0.0) / mass()
	var local_gravity := landed_on.mu / (surface_radius * surface_radius)
	if thrust_acceleration > local_gravity * 1.05 and fuel > 0.0:
		var planet := landed_on
		landed_on = null
		global_position += outward * 8.0
		velocity += outward * 3.0
		rotation = world_angle
		angular_velocity = planet.spin_speed


func _land_after_contact(planet: ProcPlanet) -> void:
	landed_on = planet
	landed_angle = (global_position - planet.global_position).angle() - planet.rotation
	surface_coordinate = SurfaceCoordinate.new(0.0, landed_angle)
	throttle = 0.0
	angular_velocity = planet.spin_speed
	_tick_landed(0.0)


func place_landed(planet: ProcPlanet) -> void:
	var outward := planet.global_position.normalized()
	if outward.length_squared() < 0.01:
		outward = Vector2.RIGHT
	_reset_resources()
	cancel_autopilot()
	landed_on = planet
	landed_angle = outward.angle() - planet.rotation
	surface_coordinate = SurfaceCoordinate.new(0.0, landed_angle)
	_tick_landed(0.0)


func can_land_at(planet: ProcPlanet) -> bool:
	if planet == null:
		return false
	var altitude := global_position.distance_to(planet.global_position) - planet.radius
	var relative_speed := (velocity - planet.inertial_velocity()).length()
	return altitude <= maxf(planet.radius * 0.35, 2500.0) and relative_speed <= 45.0


func land_at_surface(planet: ProcPlanet, coordinate: SurfaceCoordinate) -> bool:
	if not can_land_at(planet):
		return false
	cancel_autopilot()
	landed_on = planet
	surface_coordinate = coordinate.copy()
	landed_angle = surface_coordinate.longitude
	throttle = 0.0
	_tick_landed(0.0)
	return true


func engage_orbit_autopilot(target: ProcPlanet) -> void:
	if target == null:
		return
	autopilot_target = target
	autopilot_phase = "launch" if landed_on != null else "approach"
	autopilot_delta_v = 0.0
	throttle = 0.0
	time_warp = 1.0


func cancel_autopilot() -> void:
	var was_active := autopilot_target != null
	autopilot_target = null
	autopilot_phase = "off"
	autopilot_delta_v = 0.0
	if was_active:
		throttle = 0.0


func _update_autopilot_guidance() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(autopilot_target) or fuel <= 0.0:
		cancel_autopilot()
		return
	if landed_on != null:
		autopilot_phase = "launch"
		_autopilot_heading = (global_position - landed_on.global_position).angle()
		throttle = 1.0
		return
	var target := autopilot_target
	var relative_position := global_position - target.global_position
	var radius := maxf(relative_position.length(), 1.0)
	var radial := relative_position / radius
	var tangent := radial.orthogonal()
	var relative_velocity := velocity - target.inertial_velocity()
	if relative_position.cross(relative_velocity) < 0.0:
		tangent = -tangent
	var desired_radius := target.radius * 1.55
	var max_accel := MAIN_THRUST / mass()
	var desired_velocity := Vector2.ZERO
	if radius > desired_radius * 1.12:
		autopilot_phase = "approach"
		var distance_left := radius - desired_radius
		var closing_speed := minf(180.0, sqrt(maxf(2.0 * max_accel * distance_left, 0.0)) * 0.65)
		desired_velocity = target.inertial_velocity() - radial * closing_speed
	else:
		autopilot_phase = "circularize"
		var circular := OrbitalPhysics.circular_speed(target.mu, desired_radius)
		var radial_correction := clampf((radius - desired_radius) * 0.035, -35.0, 35.0)
		desired_velocity = target.inertial_velocity() + tangent * circular - radial * radial_correction
	var velocity_error := desired_velocity - velocity
	autopilot_delta_v = velocity_error.length()
	if autopilot_phase == "circularize" and autopilot_delta_v < 1.2 and absf(radius - desired_radius) < 30.0:
		autopilot_phase = "orbit"
		throttle = 0.0
		_autopilot_heading = tangent.angle()
		return
	_autopilot_heading = velocity_error.angle()
	var alignment := absf(angle_difference(rotation, _autopilot_heading))
	throttle = clampf(autopilot_delta_v / maxf(max_accel * 5.0, 1.0), 0.08, 1.0) if alignment < 0.22 else 0.0


func frame_near(planet: ProcPlanet) -> void:
	var viewport := get_viewport_rect().size
	var target_screen_radius := ProcPlanet.target_screen_radius(viewport)
	camera_zoom = clampf(
		target_screen_radius / maxf(planet.radius, 1.0),
		CAMERA_ZOOM_MIN,
		CAMERA_ZOOM_MAX
	)
	if _cam:
		_cam.global_position = global_position
		_cam.zoom = Vector2(camera_zoom, camera_zoom)


func reset_to(point: Vector2, look_at_point: Vector2, inherited_velocity: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO) -> void:
	global_position = point
	reset_physics_interpolation()
	if _cam:
		_cam.global_position = point
	velocity = inherited_velocity
	angular_velocity = 0.0
	rotation = (point - look_at_point).angle()
	landed_on = null
	cancel_autopilot()
	_reset_resources()


func _reset_resources() -> void:
	throttle = 0.0
	fuel = FUEL_MAX
	hull = HULL_MAX
	energy = ENERGY_MAX
	time_warp = 1.0


func _draw() -> void:
	if _aiming:
		var aim_local := to_local(get_global_mouse_position())
		if aim_local.length() > 35.0:
			var aim_direction := aim_local.normalized()
			var aim_end := aim_direction * minf(aim_local.length(), 900.0)
			var aim_color := Color(0.35, 1.0, 0.48, 0.72)
			draw_line(aim_direction * 30.0, aim_end, aim_color, 1.15, true)
			draw_circle(aim_end, 3.0, aim_color)
	if velocity.length() > 0.5:
		var flight_direction := velocity.normalized().rotated(-rotation)
		var line_length := clampf(velocity.length() * 0.65, 70.0, 280.0)
		var line_start := flight_direction * 30.0
		var line_end := flight_direction * line_length
		var guide_color := Color(0.35, 0.85, 1.0, 0.58)
		draw_line(line_start, line_end, guide_color, 1.2, true)
		var side := flight_direction.orthogonal()
		draw_line(line_end, line_end - flight_direction * 12.0 + side * 5.0, guide_color, 1.2, true)
		draw_line(line_end, line_end - flight_direction * 12.0 - side * 5.0, guide_color, 1.2, true)
	if fuel > 0.0 and throttle > 0.04:
		var len := 26.0 + throttle * 64.0
		var root := -22.0
		draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
			Vector2(root, -8), Vector2(root, 8), Vector2(root - len, 0),
		]), Color(0.2, 0.55, 1.0, 0.42 + throttle * 0.25))
		draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
			Vector2(root, -4.2), Vector2(root, 4.2), Vector2(root - len * 0.78, 0),
		]), Color(0.45, 0.82, 1.0, 0.8))
		draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
			Vector2(root, -1.8), Vector2(root, 1.8), Vector2(root - len * 0.5, 0),
		]), Color(0.92, 0.98, 1.0, 0.95))
	elif fuel > 0.0 and throttle < -0.04:
		var len := 10.0 + absf(throttle) * 16.0
		draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
			Vector2(30, -3), Vector2(30, 3), Vector2(30 + len, 0),
		]), Color(0.45, 0.82, 1.0, 0.55))
	var white := Color("#e8eef6")
	var pale := Color("#c5d0de")
	var red := Color("#d45a5a")
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(32, 0), Vector2(8, 8), Vector2(-18, 7), Vector2(-22, 3), Vector2(-22, -3), Vector2(-18, -7), Vector2(8, -8),
	]), white)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(10, 8), Vector2(-8, 20), Vector2(-14, 18), Vector2(-6, 7),
	]), pale)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(10, -8), Vector2(-8, -20), Vector2(-14, -18), Vector2(-6, -7),
	]), pale)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(-8, 20), Vector2(-2, 19), Vector2(-6, 14),
	]), red)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(-8, -20), Vector2(-2, -19), Vector2(-6, -14),
	]), red)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(14, 0), Vector2(4, 5), Vector2(-2, 3.5), Vector2(-2, -3.5), Vector2(4, -5),
	]), Color("#3aa0d8"))
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(12, 0), Vector2(5, 2.4), Vector2(5, -2.4),
	]), Color("#8cdcff"))
	draw_circle(Vector2(-20, 5), 3.2, Color("#9aa8b8"))
	draw_circle(Vector2(-20, -5), 3.2, Color("#9aa8b8"))

================================================================================

class_name FlightHud
extends CanvasLayer

const CYAN := Color(0.38, 0.82, 0.94, 0.95)
const CYAN_DIM := Color(0.38, 0.82, 0.94, 0.55)
const PANEL := Color(0.04, 0.08, 0.14, 0.72)
const RED := Color(0.92, 0.28, 0.32)
const BLUE := Color(0.22, 0.62, 0.95)
const ORANGE := Color(0.95, 0.62, 0.18)
const GREEN := Color(0.32, 0.86, 0.42)

var universe: Universe
var ship: SurveyShip
var system_seed: int = 0
var selected_target: ProcPlanet = null
var show_orbits: bool = false

var _paint: Control
var _font: Font


func _ready() -> void:
	layer = 10
	_font = ThemeDB.fallback_font
	_paint = Control.new()
	_paint.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	_paint.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_paint.draw.connect(_on_draw)
	add_child(_paint)
	Input.mouse_mode = Input.MOUSE_MODE_HIDDEN


func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if _paint:
		_paint.queue_redraw()


func _on_draw() -> void:
	if ship == null or universe == null:
		return
	var vp := _paint.get_viewport_rect().size
	_draw_bars()
	if ship.surface_mode_active:
		_draw_crosshair(vp)
		_draw_thrust(vp)
		_draw_hint(vp)
		return
	_draw_compass(vp)
	_draw_objective(vp)
	_draw_world_labels(vp)
	_draw_crosshair(vp)
	_draw_radar(vp)
	_draw_thrust(vp)
	_draw_modules(vp)
	_draw_hint(vp)


func _draw_bars() -> void:
	_bar(Vector2(28, 22), ship.hull / SurveyShip.HULL_MAX, RED, "%d / %d" % [int(ship.hull), int(SurveyShip.HULL_MAX)])
	_bar(Vector2(28, 46), ship.energy / SurveyShip.ENERGY_MAX, BLUE, "%d / %d" % [int(ship.energy), int(SurveyShip.ENERGY_MAX)])
	_bar(Vector2(28, 70), ship.fuel / SurveyShip.FUEL_MAX, ORANGE, "%d / %d" % [int(ship.fuel), int(SurveyShip.FUEL_MAX)])


func _bar(pos: Vector2, t: float, col: Color, txt: String) -> void:
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(pos, Vector2(168, 14)), Color(0.08, 0.12, 0.18, 0.85), true)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(pos, Vector2(168.0 * clampf(t, 0.0, 1.0), 14)), col, true)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(pos, Vector2(168, 14)), CYAN_DIM, false, 1.0)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, pos + Vector2(176, 13), txt, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, Color(0.85, 0.92, 0.98, 0.8))


func _draw_compass(vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var c := Vector2(vp.x * 0.5, 36)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(c.x - 110, 18, 220, 28), PANEL, true)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(c.x - 110, 18, 220, 28), CYAN_DIM, false, 1.0)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, c + Vector2(-102, 16), "W", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 14, CYAN)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, c + Vector2(-8, 16), "N", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 14, CYAN)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, c + Vector2(88, 16), "E", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 14, CYAN)
	var heading := wrapf(ship.rotation + PI * 0.5, -PI, PI)
	var nx := clampf(heading / PI, -1.0, 1.0) * 90.0
	_paint.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(c.x + nx, 16), Vector2(c.x + nx - 6, 8), Vector2(c.x + nx + 6, 8),
	]), CYAN)


func _draw_objective(vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var near := selected_target if selected_target else universe.nearest(ship.global_position)
	var box := Rect2(vp.x - 320, 16, 292, 72)
	_paint.draw_rect(box, PANEL, true)
	_paint.draw_rect(box, CYAN_DIM, false, 1.0)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(box.position.x + 16, box.position.y + 24), "cel", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, CYAN)
	if near:
		var dist := ship.global_position.distance_to(near.global_position) - near.radius
		var objective := "cel: %s" % near.planet_name
		if ship.landed_on != null:
			objective = "wylÄ…dowano na %s" % ship.landed_on.planet_name
		elif ship.autopilot_target != null:
			objective = "autopilot: %s" % ship.autopilot_phase
		elif ship.can_land_at(near):
			objective = "L: lÄ…duj we wskazanym miejscu"
		_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(box.position.x + 16, box.position.y + 44), objective, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 15, Color(0.9, 0.95, 1.0))
		_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(box.position.x + 16, box.position.y + 60), "odlegĹ‚oĹ›Ä‡  %s" % _fmt(dist), HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, CYAN_DIM)
	var st: Dictionary = universe.orbit_status(ship.global_position, ship.velocity)
	var orbit_txt := "%s  koĹ‚owa %.0f  ucieczki %.0f m/s" % [st["band"], st["circular_speed"], st["escape_speed"]]
	if not st["is_sun"] and st["bound"]:
		orbit_txt = "orbita %s   pe %s  ap %s" % [st["body"], _fmt(st["pe"]), _fmt(st["ap"])]
	elif st["is_sun"] and st["bound"]:
		orbit_txt = "orbita sĹ‚oĹ„ca   pe %s  ap %s" % [_fmt(st["pe"]), _fmt(st["ap"])]
	if ship.autopilot_target:
		orbit_txt = "%s   Î”v %.0f m/s" % [ship.autopilot_phase, ship.autopilot_delta_v]
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(box.position.x + 16, 96), orbit_txt, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, CYAN_DIM)


func _draw_world_labels(vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var cam := _paint.get_viewport().get_camera_2d()
	if cam == null:
		return
	for p in universe.planets:
		if p.is_moon():
			continue
		var d := ship.global_position.distance_to(p.global_position) - p.radius
		if d > 80000.0:
			continue
		var edge := p.global_position + (ship.global_position - p.global_position).normalized() * p.radius
		var scr := _world_to_screen(edge, cam, vp)
		if scr.x < 40 or scr.x > vp.x - 40 or scr.y < 90 or scr.y > vp.y - 90:
			continue
		_callout(scr, "%s\n%s" % [p.planet_name, _fmt(d)])
	for s in universe.stations:
		var d := ship.global_position.distance_to(s.global_position)
		if d > 60000.0:
			continue
		var scr := _world_to_screen(s.global_position, cam, vp)
		if scr.x < 40 or scr.x > vp.x - 40 or scr.y < 90 or scr.y > vp.y - 90:
			continue
		_callout(scr, "%s\n%s" % [s.station_name, _fmt(d)])


func _callout(scr: Vector2, txt: String) -> void:
	var box_p := scr + Vector2(18, -28)
	_paint.draw_line(scr, box_p + Vector2(0, 16), CYAN, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_circle(scr, 3.0, CYAN)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(box_p, Vector2(118, 36)), PANEL, true)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(box_p, Vector2(118, 36)), CYAN_DIM, false, 1.0)
	var lines := txt.split("\n")
	_paint.draw_string(_font, box_p + Vector2(8, 16), lines[0], HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, Color(0.92, 0.96, 1.0))
	if lines.size() > 1:
		_paint.draw_string(_font, box_p + Vector2(8, 32), lines[1], HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 12, CYAN)


func _world_to_screen(world: Vector2, cam: Camera2D, vp: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	return vp * 0.5 + (world - cam.global_position) * cam.zoom


func _draw_crosshair(_vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var c := _paint.get_local_mouse_position()
	_paint.draw_line(c + Vector2(-14, 0), c + Vector2(-4, 0), CYAN, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_line(c + Vector2(4, 0), c + Vector2(14, 0), CYAN, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_line(c + Vector2(0, -14), c + Vector2(0, -4), CYAN, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_line(c + Vector2(0, 4), c + Vector2(0, 14), CYAN, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_arc(c, 22, PI * 1.15, PI * 1.35, 8, CYAN_DIM, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_arc(c, 22, PI * 1.65, PI * 1.85, 8, CYAN_DIM, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_arc(c, 22, PI * 0.15, PI * 0.35, 8, CYAN_DIM, 1.2, true)
	_paint.draw_arc(c, 22, PI * 0.65, PI * 0.85, 8, CYAN_DIM, 1.2, true)


func _draw_radar(vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var r := 92.0
	var c := Vector2(28 + r, vp.y - 28 - r)
	var range_m := 10000.0
	_paint.draw_circle(c, r + 8, PANEL)
	_paint.draw_arc(c, r + 6, 0, TAU, 48, CYAN_DIM, 1.4, true)
	for k in 3:
		_paint.draw_arc(c, r * float(k + 1) / 3.0, 0, TAU, 40, Color(0.38, 0.82, 0.94, 0.18), 1.0, true)
	_paint.draw_line(c + Vector2(-r, 0), c + Vector2(r, 0), Color(0.38, 0.82, 0.94, 0.12), 1.0)
	_paint.draw_line(c + Vector2(0, -r), c + Vector2(0, r), Color(0.38, 0.82, 0.94, 0.12), 1.0)
	var k := r / range_m
	for p in universe.planets:
		_radar_dot(c, k, r, p.global_position, Color(0.35, 0.9, 0.4) if not p.is_moon() else Color(0.55, 0.75, 0.55))
	for s in universe.stations:
		_radar_dot(c, k, r, s.global_position, Color(0.3, 0.7, 1.0))
	for a in universe._asteroids:
		var pos: Vector2 = a["pos"]
		if ship.global_position.distance_to(pos) < range_m:
			_radar_dot(c, k, r, pos, Color(0.65, 0.68, 0.72))
	_paint.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		c + Vector2(0, -7), c + Vector2(-5, 5), c + Vector2(5, 5),
	]), Color(0.9, 0.95, 1.0))
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(c.x - r, c.y + r + 18), "zasiÄ™g  10 km", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 12, CYAN_DIM)
	var lx := c.x + r + 18
	var ly := c.y - 28
	_legend(lx, ly, Color(0.35, 0.9, 0.4), "planeta")
	_legend(lx, ly + 18, Color(0.3, 0.7, 1.0), "stacja")
	_legend(lx, ly + 36, Color(0.65, 0.68, 0.72), "asteroida")


func _radar_dot(c: Vector2, k: float, r: float, world: Vector2, col: Color) -> void:
	var rel := (world - ship.global_position) * k
	if rel.length() > r - 4.0:
		rel = rel.normalized() * (r - 4.0)
	_paint.draw_circle(c + rel, 3.2, col)


func _legend(x: float, y: float, col: Color, txt: String) -> void:
	_paint.draw_circle(Vector2(x, y), 4.0, col)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x + 12, y + 5), txt, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, Color(0.82, 0.9, 0.95, 0.8))


func _draw_thrust(vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var x := vp.x - 52.0
	var y := vp.y - 168.0
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x - 86, y + 48), "SPEED", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 12, CYAN_DIM)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x - 86, y + 74), "%d m/s" % int(ship.velocity.length()), HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 22, Color(0.92, 0.96, 1.0))
	var h := 110.0
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(x, y, 18, h), PANEL, true)
	var zero_y := y + h * 0.78
	if ship.throttle >= 0.0:
		var forward_h := h * 0.78 * clampf(ship.throttle, 0.0, 1.0)
		_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(x, zero_y - forward_h, 18, forward_h), GREEN, true)
	else:
		var reverse_h := h * 0.22 * clampf(absf(ship.throttle) / 0.28, 0.0, 1.0)
		_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(x, zero_y, 18, reverse_h), ORANGE, true)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(x, y, 18, h), CYAN_DIM, false, 1.0)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x + 24, y + 12), "MAX", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, CYAN_DIM)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x + 24, y + h * 0.5), "1/2", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, CYAN_DIM)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x + 24, zero_y + 4), "STOP", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, CYAN_DIM)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x + 24, y + h - 2), "REV", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, CYAN_DIM)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(x - 8, y + h + 18), "THRUST", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, CYAN_DIM)


func _draw_modules(vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var y := vp.y - 92.0
	var x := vp.x * 0.5 - 182.0
	_module(Vector2(x, y), "SAS", "on" if ship.sas else "off", ship.sas)
	_module(Vector2(x + 92, y), "WARP", "Ă—%.0f" % ship.time_warp, ship.time_warp > 1.2)
	_module(Vector2(x + 184, y), "LÄ„D", "tak" if ship.landed_on else "nie", ship.landed_on != null)
	_module(Vector2(x + 276, y), "ORBITA", ship.autopilot_phase if ship.autopilot_target else ("widok" if show_orbits else "off"), ship.autopilot_target != null or show_orbits)


func _module(pos: Vector2, title: String, val: String, on: bool) -> void:
	var col := CYAN if on else CYAN_DIM
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(pos, Vector2(80, 58)), PANEL, true)
	_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(pos, Vector2(80, 58)), col, false, 1.2)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, pos + Vector2(10, 24), title, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, col)
	_paint.draw_string(_font, pos + Vector2(10, 46), val, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 15, Color(0.9, 0.95, 1.0))


func _draw_hint(vp: Vector2) -> void:
	var hint := "w/s ciÄ…g  a/d obrĂłt  q/e rcs  kĂłĹ‚ko/+/- zoom  0 kadr  o orbity  tab cel  f autopilot"
	if ship.surface_mode_active:
		hint = "lÄ…dowanie: w ciÄ…g  strzaĹ‚ki rcs  kĂłĹ‚ko/+/- zoom  0 reset"
		if ship.landed_on:
			hint = "lot nad powierzchniÄ…: wasd/strzaĹ‚ki ciÄ…g  spacja start  bezwĹ‚adnoĹ›Ä‡ aktywna"
	_paint.draw_string(_font, Vector2(28, vp.y - 14), hint, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 12, Color(0.55, 0.7, 0.8, 0.45))


func _fmt(meters: float) -> String:
	if absf(meters) >= 1000000.0:
		return "%.2f Mm" % (meters / 1000000.0)
	if absf(meters) >= 1000.0:
		return "%.1f km" % (meters / 1000.0)
	return "%.0f m" % meters



##### backup-units-20260915-175616\scripts\surface_mode.gd #####
class_name SurfaceMode
extends CanvasLayer

const SURFACE_GLOBE_SHADER := preload("res://shaders/surface_globe.gdshader")

enum SurfaceState {
	INACTIVE,
	LANDING,
	GROUNDED,
	ASCENDING,
}

const SKY := Color("#070b18")
const HORIZON := Color("#16344a")
const LOCAL_GRAVITY := 12.0
const LOCAL_THRUST := 27.0
const HOVER_ALTITUDE := 14.0
const SURFACE_CIRCUIT_TIME_AT_MAX_SPEED := 240.0
const HOVER_ACCELERATION_TIME := 2.5
const HOVER_DRAG := 0.16
const SURFACE_ZOOM_MIN := 0.25
const SURFACE_ZOOM_MAX := 6.0
const SURFACE_ZOOM_STEP := 1.25
const SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT := 1.0
const HORIZON_VISIBILITY_EPSILON := 0.015
const HORIZON_OBJECT_SCALE := 0.45
const HORIZON_FLATTEN_MIN := 0.12
const MAX_SAFE_LANDING_RADIAL_SPEED := 42.0
const MAX_SAFE_LANDING_TANGENT_SPEED := 48.0
const AUTO_ENTRY_ALTITUDE := 420.0
const EXIT_ALTITUDE := 460.0

var ship: SurveyShip
var _sky_paint: Control
var _globe_visual: ColorRect
var _paint: Control
var _font: Font
var _active_planet: ProcPlanet = null
var _state: SurfaceState = SurfaceState.INACTIVE
var _blend: float = 0.0
var _entry_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _entry_coordinate := SurfaceCoordinate.new()
var player_surface_position: Vector3 = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
var planet_view_rotation: Quaternion = Quaternion.IDENTITY
var surface_zoom: float = SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT
var _altitude: float = 0.0
var _radial_velocity: float = 0.0
var _surface_velocity: Vector3 = Vector3.ZERO
var _ground_heading: Vector2 = Vector2.RIGHT


func _ready() -> void:
	layer = 5
	_font = ThemeDB.fallback_font
	_sky_paint = Control.new()
	_sky_paint.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	_sky_paint.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_sky_paint.visible = false
	_sky_paint.draw.connect(_draw_surface_background)
	add_child(_sky_paint)
	_globe_visual = ColorRect.new()
	_globe_visual.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_globe_visual.visible = false
	var globe_material := ShaderMaterial.new()
	globe_material.shader = SURFACE_GLOBE_SHADER
	_globe_visual.material = globe_material
	add_child(_globe_visual)
	_paint = Control.new()
	_paint.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	_paint.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_paint.visible = false
	_paint.draw.connect(_draw_surface)
	add_child(_paint)


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if _state == SurfaceState.INACTIVE:
		return
	var previous_zoom := surface_zoom
	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed:
		if event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
			surface_zoom = clampf(surface_zoom * SURFACE_ZOOM_STEP, SURFACE_ZOOM_MIN, SURFACE_ZOOM_MAX)
		elif event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
			surface_zoom = clampf(surface_zoom / SURFACE_ZOOM_STEP, SURFACE_ZOOM_MIN, SURFACE_ZOOM_MAX)
	elif event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		match event.physical_keycode:
			KEY_EQUAL, KEY_KP_ADD:
				surface_zoom = clampf(surface_zoom * SURFACE_ZOOM_STEP, SURFACE_ZOOM_MIN, SURFACE_ZOOM_MAX)
			KEY_MINUS, KEY_KP_SUBTRACT:
				surface_zoom = clampf(surface_zoom / SURFACE_ZOOM_STEP, SURFACE_ZOOM_MIN, SURFACE_ZOOM_MAX)
			KEY_0, KEY_KP_0:
				surface_zoom = SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT
	if is_equal_approx(previous_zoom, surface_zoom):
		return
	_update_surface_camera()


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	var target_blend := 1.0 if _state != SurfaceState.INACTIVE else 0.0
	_blend = move_toward(_blend, target_blend, delta * 4.0)
	var visible := _blend > 0.001
	var opacity := smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, _blend)
	if _sky_paint:
		_sky_paint.visible = visible
		_sky_paint.modulate.a = opacity
		_sky_paint.queue_redraw()
	if _globe_visual:
		_globe_visual.visible = visible
		_globe_visual.modulate.a = opacity
		_update_globe_visual()
	if _paint:
		_paint.visible = visible
		_paint.modulate.a = opacity
		_paint.queue_redraw()


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	_entry_cooldown = maxf(0.0, _entry_cooldown - delta)
	if _state == SurfaceState.INACTIVE:
		_try_auto_enter()
		return
	if not is_instance_valid(_active_planet) or ship == null:
		_leave_surface_mode()
		return
	if ship.landed_on != null and ship.landed_on != _active_planet:
		begin_grounded(ship.landed_on)
		return
	_tick_surface(delta)


func begin_landing(planet: ProcPlanet, coordinate: SurfaceCoordinate) -> bool:
	if ship == null or planet == null or _state != SurfaceState.INACTIVE:
		return false
	if not ship.can_land_at(planet):
		return false
	_enter_landing(planet, coordinate)
	return true


func begin_grounded(planet: ProcPlanet) -> void:
	if ship == null or planet == null:
		return
	_active_planet = planet
	_entry_coordinate = ship.surface_coordinate.copy()
	player_surface_position = _entry_coordinate.to_unit_vector()
	_center_globe_on_player()
	_altitude = HOVER_ALTITUDE
	_radial_velocity = 0.0
	_surface_velocity = Vector3.ZERO
	_ground_heading = Vector2.RIGHT
	_state = SurfaceState.GROUNDED
	surface_zoom = SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT
	ship.surface_mode_active = true
	ship.landed_on = planet
	ship.throttle = 0.0
	_sync_world_proxy()


func _try_auto_enter() -> void:
	if ship == null or ship.universe == null or _entry_cooldown > 0.0:
		return
	if ship.landed_on != null:
		begin_grounded(ship.landed_on)
		return
	var planet := ship.universe.nearest(ship.global_position)
	if planet == null:
		return
	var altitude := ship.global_position.distance_to(planet.global_position) - planet.radius - SurveyShip.HULL_R
	if altitude <= AUTO_ENTRY_ALTITUDE:
		_enter_landing(planet, planet.surface_from_world(ship.global_position))


func _enter_landing(planet: ProcPlanet, coordinate: SurfaceCoordinate) -> void:
	_active_planet = planet
	_entry_coordinate = coordinate.copy()
	player_surface_position = coordinate.to_unit_vector()
	_center_globe_on_player()
	surface_zoom = SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT
	var actual_altitude := maxf(
		ship.global_position.distance_to(planet.global_position) - planet.radius - SurveyShip.HULL_R,
		0.0
	)
	_altitude = clampf(actual_altitude, 180.0, 340.0)
	var world_angle := coordinate.longitude + planet.rotation
	var outward := Vector2.from_angle(world_angle)
	var tangent := outward.orthogonal()
	var relative_velocity := ship.velocity - planet.inertial_velocity()
	var east := _east_at(player_surface_position)
	_surface_velocity = east * clampf(relative_velocity.dot(tangent), -75.0, 75.0)
	_radial_velocity = clampf(relative_velocity.dot(outward), -52.0, 18.0)
	_ground_heading = Vector2.RIGHT
	_state = SurfaceState.LANDING
	ship.cancel_autopilot()
	ship.surface_mode_active = true
	ship.landed_on = null
	ship.throttle = 0.0
	_sync_world_proxy()


func _tick_surface(delta: float) -> void:
	if _state == SurfaceState.GROUNDED:
		_tick_grounded(delta)
	else:
		_tick_flight(delta)
	_update_surface_camera_follow(delta)
	_sync_world_proxy()


func _tick_grounded(delta: float) -> void:
	var movement := _surface_movement_input()
	if movement.length_squared() > 0.01:
		movement = movement.normalized()
		_ground_heading = movement
		_surface_velocity += _screen_direction_to_tangent(movement) * _hover_acceleration() * delta
	var max_hover_speed := _hover_max_speed()
	if _surface_velocity.length() > max_hover_speed:
		_surface_velocity = _surface_velocity.normalized() * max_hover_speed
	_surface_velocity *= exp(-HOVER_DRAG * delta)
	_altitude = HOVER_ALTITUDE
	_radial_velocity = 0.0
	ship.throttle = 0.0
	_advance_surface_velocity(delta)
	var view_velocity := planet_view_rotation * _surface_velocity
	var screen_velocity := Vector2(view_velocity.x, -view_velocity.y)
	if screen_velocity.length_squared() > 1.0:
		_ground_heading = screen_velocity.normalized()
	if Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_SPACE):
		_state = SurfaceState.ASCENDING
		ship.landed_on = null
		ship.throttle = 1.0
		_altitude = HOVER_ALTITUDE + 5.0
		_radial_velocity = 24.0
		_surface_velocity = Vector3.ZERO


func _tick_flight(delta: float) -> void:
	var lateral_input := Vector2(
		float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_RIGHT)) - float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_LEFT)),
		float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_DOWN)) - float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_UP))
	)
	if lateral_input.length_squared() > 0.01:
		lateral_input = lateral_input.normalized()
		_ground_heading = lateral_input
		var tangent_acceleration := _screen_direction_to_tangent(lateral_input) * 24.0
		_surface_velocity += tangent_acceleration * delta
	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		var ship_screen := _ship_screen_position()
		var aim := _paint.get_local_mouse_position() - ship_screen
		if aim.length_squared() > 16.0:
			_ground_heading = aim.normalized()
	var engine_on := (
		Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_W)
		or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_SHIFT)
	)
	ship.throttle = move_toward(ship.throttle, 1.0 if engine_on else 0.0, delta * 3.5)
	_radial_velocity -= LOCAL_GRAVITY * delta
	if ship.throttle > 0.01 and ship.fuel > 0.0:
		_radial_velocity += LOCAL_THRUST * ship.throttle * delta
		ship.fuel = maxf(0.0, ship.fuel - ship.throttle * 1.1 * delta)
	_surface_velocity *= exp(-0.2 * delta)
	_advance_surface_velocity(delta)
	_altitude += _radial_velocity * delta
	if _altitude <= 0.0:
		_resolve_ground_contact()
	if _state == SurfaceState.ASCENDING and _altitude >= EXIT_ALTITUDE:
		_finish_launch()


func _resolve_ground_contact() -> void:
	var impact_speed := maxf(-_radial_velocity, 0.0)
	if (
		impact_speed <= MAX_SAFE_LANDING_RADIAL_SPEED
		and _surface_velocity.length() <= MAX_SAFE_LANDING_TANGENT_SPEED
	):
		_altitude = HOVER_ALTITUDE
		_radial_velocity = 0.0
		_surface_velocity *= 0.35
		_state = SurfaceState.GROUNDED
		surface_zoom = SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT
		ship.landed_on = _active_planet
		ship.throttle = 0.0
		return
	ship.hull = maxf(0.0, ship.hull - maxf(impact_speed - 18.0, 4.0) * 0.85)
	_altitude = 2.0
	_radial_velocity = maxf(10.0, impact_speed * 0.28)
	_surface_velocity *= 0.55


func _finish_launch() -> void:
	var planet := _active_planet
	var coordinate := _current_coordinate()
	var world_angle := coordinate.longitude + planet.rotation
	var outward := Vector2.from_angle(world_angle)
	var tangent := outward.orthogonal()
	var outward_speed := maxf(_radial_velocity, 28.0)
	var tangent_speed := _surface_velocity.dot(_east_at(player_surface_position))
	ship.global_position = planet.global_position + outward * (
		planet.radius + SurveyShip.HULL_R + EXIT_ALTITUDE + 60.0
	)
	ship.velocity = planet.inertial_velocity() + tangent * tangent_speed + outward * outward_speed
	ship.rotation = outward.angle()
	ship.surface_coordinate = coordinate
	ship.landed_on = null
	ship.surface_mode_active = false
	ship.throttle = maxf(ship.throttle, 0.75)
	ship.reset_physics_interpolation()
	ship.frame_near(planet)
	_active_planet = null
	_state = SurfaceState.INACTIVE
	_entry_cooldown = 2.0


func _leave_surface_mode() -> void:
	if ship:
		ship.surface_mode_active = false
	_active_planet = null
	_state = SurfaceState.INACTIVE
	_entry_cooldown = 1.0


func _sync_world_proxy() -> void:
	if ship == null or not is_instance_valid(_active_planet):
		return
	var coordinate := _current_coordinate()
	var world_angle := coordinate.longitude + _active_planet.rotation
	var outward := Vector2.from_angle(world_angle)
	var tangent := outward.orthogonal()
	ship.surface_coordinate = coordinate
	ship.global_position = _active_planet.global_position + outward * (
		_active_planet.radius + SurveyShip.HULL_R + _altitude
	)
	ship.rotation = outward.angle()
	if _state == SurfaceState.GROUNDED:
		var surface_radius := _active_planet.radius + SurveyShip.HULL_R
		ship.velocity = (
			_active_planet.inertial_velocity()
			+ tangent * _active_planet.spin_speed * surface_radius
		)
	else:
		var east_speed := _surface_velocity.dot(_east_at(player_surface_position))
		ship.velocity = (
			_active_planet.inertial_velocity()
			+ tangent * east_speed
			+ outward * _radial_velocity
		)


func _center_globe_on_player() -> void:
	planet_view_rotation = Quaternion(
		player_surface_position.normalized(),
		Vector3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
	).normalized()


func _surface_movement_input() -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(
		float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_D) or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_RIGHT))
			- float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_A) or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_LEFT)),
		float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_S) or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_DOWN))
			- float(Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_W) or Input.is_physical_key_pressed(KEY_UP))
	)


func _screen_direction_to_tangent(screen_direction: Vector2) -> Vector3:
	if screen_direction.length_squared() < 0.0001:
		return Vector3.ZERO
	var view_direction := Vector3(screen_direction.x, -screen_direction.y, 0.0).normalized()
	var planet_direction := planet_view_rotation.inverse() * view_direction
	var tangent := planet_direction - player_surface_position * planet_direction.dot(player_surface_position)
	return tangent.normalized()


func _move_player_on_sphere(screen_direction: Vector2, angular_distance: float) -> void:
	var tangent := _screen_direction_to_tangent(screen_direction)
	if tangent.length_squared() < 0.0001:
		return
	player_surface_position = (
		player_surface_position * cos(angular_distance)
		+ tangent * sin(angular_distance)
	).normalized()


func _apply_surface_movement(direction: Vector2, distance: float) -> void:
	_move_player_on_sphere(direction, _surface_angular_distance(distance))
	_update_surface_camera()


func _advance_surface_velocity(delta: float) -> void:
	var speed := _surface_velocity.length()
	if speed < 0.001:
		return
	var tangent := (
		_surface_velocity
		- player_surface_position * _surface_velocity.dot(player_surface_position)
	).normalized()
	var angular_distance := _surface_angular_distance(speed * delta)
	player_surface_position = (
		player_surface_position * cos(angular_distance)
		+ tangent * sin(angular_distance)
	).normalized()
	_surface_velocity = (
		_surface_velocity
		- player_surface_position * _surface_velocity.dot(player_surface_position)
	).normalized() * speed
	_update_surface_camera()


func _update_surface_camera() -> void:
	var view_position := planet_view_rotation * player_surface_position
	_orient_player_at_disk_position(view_position, Vector2.ZERO)


func _update_surface_camera_follow(_delta: float) -> void:
	_update_surface_camera()


func _orient_player_at_disk_position(view_position: Vector3, desired_disk: Vector2) -> void:
	planet_view_rotation = _rotation_placing_player_at_disk_position(view_position, desired_disk)


func _rotation_placing_player_at_disk_position(
	view_position: Vector3,
	desired_disk: Vector2
) -> Quaternion:
	desired_disk = desired_disk.limit_length(0.98)
	var desired_view := Vector3(
		desired_disk.x,
		-desired_disk.y,
		sqrt(maxf(1.0 - desired_disk.length_squared(), 0.0))
	)
	var correction := Quaternion(view_position.normalized(), desired_view.normalized())
	return (correction * planet_view_rotation).normalized()


func _east_at(point: Vector3) -> Vector3:
	var east := point.cross(Vector3.UP)
	if east.length_squared() < 0.0001:
		east = point.cross(Vector3.FORWARD)
	return east.normalized()


func _surface_angular_distance(linear_distance: float) -> float:
	if not is_instance_valid(_active_planet):
		return 0.0
	return linear_distance / maxf(_active_planet.radius + _altitude, 1.0)


func _hover_max_speed() -> float:
	if not is_instance_valid(_active_planet):
		return 0.0
	var local_radius := maxf(_active_planet.radius + _altitude, 1.0)
	return TAU * local_radius / SURFACE_CIRCUIT_TIME_AT_MAX_SPEED


func _hover_acceleration() -> float:
	return _hover_max_speed() / HOVER_ACCELERATION_TIME


func _altitude_meters() -> float:
	return maxf(_altitude, 0.0)


func _current_coordinate() -> SurfaceCoordinate:
	if _active_planet == null:
		return _entry_coordinate.copy()
	return SurfaceCoordinate.from_unit_vector(player_surface_position)


func _ship_screen_position() -> Vector2:
	var viewport := _paint.get_viewport_rect().size
	return _project_surface_point(player_surface_position, viewport, _altitude)["position"]


func _project_surface_point(
	surface_point: Vector3,
	viewport: Vector2,
	altitude: float = 0.0
) -> Dictionary:
	var viewed := planet_view_rotation * surface_point.normalized()
	var depth := clampf(viewed.z, 0.0, 1.0)
	var radius_factor := 1.0
	if is_instance_valid(_active_planet):
		radius_factor += maxf(altitude, 0.0) / maxf(_active_planet.radius, 1.0)
	var center := _planet_center(viewport)
	var disk_offset := Vector2(viewed.x, -viewed.y) * _planet_radius(viewport)
	return {
		"visible": viewed.z > HORIZON_VISIBILITY_EPSILON,
		"position": center + disk_offset * radius_factor,
		"surface_position": center + disk_offset,
		"view": viewed,
		"depth": depth,
		"scale": lerpf(HORIZON_OBJECT_SCALE, 1.0, depth),
		"flatten": lerpf(HORIZON_FLATTEN_MIN, 1.0, depth),
		"radius_factor": radius_factor,
	}


func _planet_center(viewport: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(viewport.x * 0.5, viewport.y * 0.56)


func _planet_radius(viewport: Vector2) -> float:
	return ProcPlanet.target_screen_radius(viewport) * surface_zoom


func _draw_surface() -> void:
	if _active_planet == null or ship == null:
		return
	var viewport := _paint.get_viewport_rect().size
	_draw_ground(viewport)
	var ship_projection := _project_surface_point(player_surface_position, viewport, _altitude)
	if ship_projection["visible"]:
		_draw_local_ship(ship_projection)
	_draw_surface_readout(viewport)


func _draw_surface_background() -> void:
	_draw_sky(_sky_paint.get_viewport_rect().size)


func _draw_sky(viewport: Vector2) -> void:
	_sky_paint.draw_rect(Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, viewport), SKY, true)
	for i in 8:
		var t := float(i) / 8.0
		var color := HORIZON
		color.a = 0.16 * (1.0 - t)
		_sky_paint.draw_rect(
			Rect2(Vector2(0, viewport.y * (0.47 + t * 0.035)), Vector2(viewport.x, viewport.y * 0.09)),
			color,
			true
		)
	for i in 64:
		var x := fmod(float(i * 193 + 47), viewport.x)
		var y := fmod(float(i * 79 + 31), viewport.y * 0.58)
		var size := 1.0 + float(i % 3) * 0.45
		_sky_paint.draw_circle(Vector2(x, y), size, Color(0.82, 0.9, 1.0, 0.45 + float(i % 4) * 0.1))


func _update_globe_visual() -> void:
	if _active_planet == null or _globe_visual == null:
		return
	var viewport := _globe_visual.get_viewport_rect().size
	var center := _planet_center(viewport)
	var radius := _planet_radius(viewport)
	_globe_visual.position = center - Vector2.ONE * radius
	_globe_visual.size = Vector2.ONE * radius * 2.0
	var material := _globe_visual.material as ShaderMaterial
	if material == null:
		return
	var palette := _active_planet.surface_palette()
	material.set_shader_parameter("inverse_view_rotation", Basis(planet_view_rotation).inverse())
	material.set_shader_parameter("deep_color", palette["deep"])
	material.set_shader_parameter("low_color", palette["low"])
	material.set_shader_parameter("mid_color", palette["mid"])
	material.set_shader_parameter("high_color", palette["high"])
	material.set_shader_parameter("ice_color", palette["ice"])
	material.set_shader_parameter("ocean_level", palette["ocean_level"])
	material.set_shader_parameter("seed", _active_planet.seed_value)


func _draw_ground(viewport: Vector2) -> void:
	var center := _planet_center(viewport)
	var radius := _planet_radius(viewport)
	var base := _active_planet.map_color()
	_paint.draw_arc(center, radius + 6.0, 0.0, TAU, 192, Color(base.lightened(0.35), 0.12), 8.0, true)
	for layer_index in 4:
		var layer_radius := radius * (0.82 - float(layer_index) * 0.12)
		var layer_color := base.darkened(0.28 + float(layer_index) * 0.08)
		layer_color.a = 0.1
		_paint.draw_arc(center, layer_radius, 0.0, TAU, 128, layer_color, 2.0, true)
	_paint.draw_arc(center, radius, 0.0, TAU, 192, base.lightened(0.42), 3.0, true)


func _draw_local_ship(projection: Dictionary) -> void:
	var center: Vector2 = projection["position"]
	var zoom_scale := surface_zoom
	var view_velocity := planet_view_rotation * _surface_velocity
	var screen_velocity := Vector2(view_velocity.x, -view_velocity.y)
	if screen_velocity.length() > 1.0:
		var direction := screen_velocity.normalized()
		var finish := center + direction * clampf(
			screen_velocity.length() * 1.1 * zoom_scale,
			65.0 * zoom_scale,
			240.0 * zoom_scale
		)
		_paint.draw_line(
			center + direction * 36.0 * zoom_scale,
			finish,
			Color(0.35, 0.85, 1.0, 0.58),
			1.2 * zoom_scale,
			true
		)
	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		var cursor := _paint.get_local_mouse_position()
		var aim := cursor - center
		if aim.length() > 38.0 * zoom_scale:
			_paint.draw_line(
				center + aim.normalized() * 38.0 * zoom_scale,
				cursor,
				Color(0.35, 1.0, 0.48, 0.7),
				1.15 * zoom_scale,
				true
			)
	if _altitude > 1.0:
		var shadow_alpha := lerpf(0.28, 0.06, clampf(_altitude / EXIT_ALTITUDE, 0.0, 1.0))
		_paint.draw_circle(
			Vector2(projection["surface_position"]) + Vector2(8.0, 9.0) * zoom_scale,
			22.0 * float(projection["scale"]) * zoom_scale,
			Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, shadow_alpha)
		)
	var ship_scale := _ship_visual_scale(projection)
	_paint.draw_set_transform(center, _ground_heading.angle(), ship_scale)
	_draw_local_ship_body()


func _ship_visual_scale(projection: Dictionary) -> Vector2:
	var altitude_scale := clampf(1.12 + _altitude / 1100.0, 1.12, 1.58)
	var perspective_scale: float = projection["scale"]
	var horizon_flatten: float = projection["flatten"]
	var scaled_size := altitude_scale * perspective_scale * surface_zoom
	return Vector2(
		scaled_size,
		scaled_size * horizon_flatten
	)


func _draw_local_ship_body() -> void:
	if ship.throttle > 0.04 and ship.fuel > 0.0:
		var flame := 25.0 + ship.throttle * 42.0
		_paint.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
			Vector2(-22, -6), Vector2(-22, 6), Vector2(-22 - flame, 0),
		]), Color(0.3, 0.72, 1.0, 0.8))
	_paint.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(34, 0), Vector2(8, 9), Vector2(-20, 7), Vector2(-24, 0), Vector2(-20, -7), Vector2(8, -9),
	]), Color("#e8eef6"))
	_paint.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(9, 9), Vector2(-10, 21), Vector2(-7, 8),
	]), Color("#c5d0de"))
	_paint.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(9, -9), Vector2(-10, -21), Vector2(-7, -8),
	]), Color("#c5d0de"))
	_paint.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(15, 0), Vector2(4, 5), Vector2(-3, 3), Vector2(-3, -3), Vector2(4, -5),
	]), Color("#3aa0d8"))
	if _state == SurfaceState.GROUNDED:
		var pulse := 0.55 + sin(Time.get_ticks_msec() * 0.008) * 0.15
		_paint.draw_circle(Vector2(-17.0, -7.0), 4.5, Color(0.3, 0.75, 1.0, pulse))
		_paint.draw_circle(Vector2(-17.0, 7.0), 4.5, Color(0.3, 0.75, 1.0, pulse))
	_paint.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2.ONE)


func _draw_surface_readout(viewport: Vector2) -> void:
	var state_text := "LÄ„DOWANIE"
	if _state == SurfaceState.GROUNDED:
		state_text = "LOT NAD POWIERZCHNIÄ„"
	elif _state == SurfaceState.ASCENDING:
		state_text = "START"
	var coordinate := _current_coordinate()
	var title := "%s   wysokoĹ›Ä‡ %.0f m   szer. %+.2fÂ°   dĹ‚. %+.2fÂ°   zoom %.0f%%" % [
		state_text,
		_altitude_meters(),
		rad_to_deg(coordinate.latitude),
		rad_to_deg(coordinate.longitude),
		surface_zoom * 100.0,
	]
	_paint.draw_string(
		_font,
		Vector2(viewport.x * 0.5 - 260.0, 72.0),
		title,
		HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER,
		520.0,
		16,
		Color(0.66, 0.88, 0.98, 0.88)
	)
	var controls := "W ciÄ…g   strzaĹ‚ki RCS   kĂłĹ‚ko/+/- zoom   0 reset"
	if _state == SurfaceState.GROUNDED:
		controls = "WASD/strzaĹ‚ki ciÄ…g boczny   SPACJA start w kosmos   ruch ma bezwĹ‚adnoĹ›Ä‡"
	_paint.draw_string(
		_font,
		Vector2(viewport.x * 0.5 - 220.0, 96.0),
		controls,
		HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER,
		440.0,
		14,
		Color(0.55, 0.76, 0.88, 0.72)
	)

================================================================================
PLIK: run_tests.gd
ĹšCIEĹ»KA: C:\autobus\tests\run_tests.gd
================================================================================
extends Node

const OrbitalPhysicsType := preload("res://scripts/orbital_physics.gd")
const SurfaceCoordinateType := preload("res://scripts/surface_coordinate.gd")
const UniverseType := preload("res://scripts/universe.gd")
const PlanetType := preload("res://scripts/planet.gd")
const ShipType := preload("res://scripts/ship.gd")
const SurfaceModeType := preload("res://scripts/surface_mode.gd")

var passed := 0
var failed := 0


func _ready() -> void:
	print("Horizon physics tests")
	_test_orbital_speeds()
	_test_orbital_elements()
	_test_orbit_stability()
	_test_surface_coordinates()
	_test_horizon()
	_test_generated_orbits()
	_test_autopilot_is_physical()
	_test_landing_coordinates()
	_test_liftoff_threshold()
	_test_manual_throttle_survives_autopilot_cancel()
	_test_surface_minigame_flow()
	print("passed %d  failed %d" % [passed, failed])
	get_tree().quit(1 if failed > 0 else 0)


func _check(condition: bool, label: String) -> void:
	if condition:
		passed += 1
		print("  ok   ", label)
	else:
		failed += 1
		print("  FAIL ", label)


func _test_orbital_speeds() -> void:
	var mu := OrbitalPhysicsType.mu_from_surface_gravity(10.0, 1000.0)
	var circular := OrbitalPhysicsType.circular_speed(mu, 1500.0)
	var escape := OrbitalPhysicsType.escape_speed(mu, 1500.0)
	_check(absf(escape / circular - sqrt(2.0)) < 0.0001, "escape speed is sqrt(2) times circular")
	_check(absf(mu - 10000000.0) < 0.1, "mu = surface gravity times radius squared")


func _test_orbital_elements() -> void:
	var mu := 10000000.0
	var radius := 1500.0
	var velocity := Vector2(0.0, OrbitalPhysicsType.circular_speed(mu, radius))
	var elements := OrbitalPhysicsType.elements(Vector2(radius, 0.0), velocity, mu)
	_check(elements["bound"], "circular orbit is bound")
	_check(elements["e"] < 0.0001, "circular orbit eccentricity is near zero")
	var escape_velocity := Vector2(0.0, OrbitalPhysicsType.escape_speed(mu, radius) * 1.001)
	var escape_elements := OrbitalPhysicsType.elements(Vector2(radius, 0.0), escape_velocity, mu)
	_check(not escape_elements["bound"], "speed above escape is unbound")


func _test_orbit_stability() -> void:
	var mu := 10000000.0
	var radius := 1500.0
	var position := Vector2(radius, 0.0)
	var velocity := Vector2(0.0, OrbitalPhysicsType.circular_speed(mu, radius))
	var period := OrbitalPhysicsType.orbital_period(mu, radius)
	var dt := 1.0 / 120.0
	var steps := int(period / dt)
	for i in steps:
		velocity += OrbitalPhysicsType.gravity_at(position, Vector2.ZERO, mu) * dt
		position += velocity * dt
	_check(absf(position.length() - radius) / radius < 0.01, "symplectic orbit keeps radius for one period")


func _test_surface_coordinates() -> void:
	var original := SurfaceCoordinateType.new(0.61, -2.18)
	var restored := SurfaceCoordinateType.from_unit_vector(original.to_unit_vector())
	_check(absf(original.latitude - restored.latitude) < 0.00001, "latitude roundtrip")
	_check(absf(angle_difference(original.longitude, restored.longitude)) < 0.00001, "longitude roundtrip")
	var wrapped := SurfaceCoordinateType.new(0.0, PI * 3.0)
	_check(absf(absf(wrapped.longitude) - PI) < 0.00001, "longitude wraps around globe")


func _test_horizon() -> void:
	var observer := SurfaceCoordinateType.new(0.0, 0.0)
	var near := SurfaceCoordinateType.new(0.2, 0.3)
	var far := SurfaceCoordinateType.new(0.0, PI)
	_check(near.is_above_horizon_for(observer), "near surface site is visible")
	_check(not far.is_above_horizon_for(observer), "far side site is hidden")


func _test_generated_orbits() -> void:
	var universe := UniverseType.new()
	add_child(universe)
	universe.generate(12345)
	var correct := true
	for planet in universe.planets:
		var parent_mu := universe.sun_mu()
		if planet.parent_body:
			parent_mu = planet.parent_body.mu
		var expected := OrbitalPhysicsType.circular_speed(parent_mu, planet.orbit_radius) / planet.orbit_radius
		if absf(absf(planet.orbit_speed) - expected) / maxf(expected, 0.000001) > 0.001:
			correct = false
	_check(correct, "generated planets and moons use physical circular speed")
	universe.queue_free()


func _test_autopilot_is_physical() -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 9
	var planet := PlanetType.new()
	planet.configure(rng, "Test", "arid", 6000.0, 100000.0)
	add_child(planet)
	var ship := ShipType.new()
	add_child(ship)
	ship.global_position = planet.global_position + Vector2(planet.radius * 2.0, 0.0)
	var before := ship.global_position
	ship.engage_orbit_autopilot(planet)
	_check(ship.global_position.distance_to(before) < 0.001, "autopilot does not teleport ship")
	ship._update_autopilot_guidance()
	ship.rotation = ship._autopilot_heading
	ship._update_autopilot_guidance()
	var fuel_before: float = ship.fuel
	ship._apply_forces(1.0)
	_check(ship.fuel < fuel_before, "autopilot maneuver consumes fuel")
	planet.queue_free()
	ship.queue_free()


func _test_landing_coordinates() -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 12
	var planet := PlanetType.new()
	planet.configure(rng, "Landing", "ocean", 7000.0, 120000.0)
	add_child(planet)
	var ship := ShipType.new()
	add_child(ship)
	ship.global_position = planet.global_position + Vector2(planet.radius + 100.0, 0.0)
	ship.velocity = planet.inertial_velocity()
	var destination := SurfaceCoordinateType.new(0.72, -1.35)
	var landed: bool = ship.land_at_surface(planet, destination)
	_check(landed, "safe nearby ship can land")
	_check(absf(ship.surface_coordinate.latitude - destination.latitude) < 0.0001, "landing preserves latitude")
	_check(absf(angle_difference(ship.surface_coordinate.longitude, destination.longitude)) < 0.0001, "landing preserves longitude")
	planet.queue_free()
	ship.queue_free()


func _test_liftoff_threshold() -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 22
	var planet := PlanetType.new()
	planet.configure(rng, "Launch", "arid", 7000.0, 120000.0)
	add_child(planet)
	var ship := ShipType.new()
	add_child(ship)
	ship.place_landed(planet)
	ship.throttle = 0.2
	ship._tick_landed(1.0 / 60.0)
	_check(ship.landed_on == planet, "low thrust does not release landing contact")
	ship.throttle = 1.0
	ship._tick_landed(1.0 / 60.0)
	_check(ship.landed_on == null, "full thrust lifts off from planet")
	planet.queue_free()
	ship.queue_free()


func _test_manual_throttle_survives_autopilot_cancel() -> void:
	var ship := ShipType.new()
	add_child(ship)
	ship.throttle = 0.7
	ship.cancel_autopilot()
	_check(is_equal_approx(ship.throttle, 0.7), "manual throttle is not reset without active autopilot")
	ship.queue_free()


func _test_surface_minigame_flow() -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 31
	var planet := PlanetType.new()
	planet.configure(rng, "Minigame", "ocean", 7000.0, 120000.0)
	add_child(planet)
	var ship := ShipType.new()
	add_child(ship)
	ship.place_landed(planet)
	var surface := SurfaceModeType.new()
	surface.ship = ship
	add_child(surface)
	surface.begin_grounded(planet)
	_check(ship.surface_mode_active and ship.landed_on == planet, "surface minigame starts in grounded state")
	_check(is_equal_approx(surface._altitude, SurfaceModeType.HOVER_ALTITUDE), "surface ship hovers above the ground")
	var centered_view: Vector3 = surface.planet_view_rotation * surface.player_surface_position
	_check(
		Vector2(centered_view.x, centered_view.y).length() < 0.0001 and centered_view.z > 0.9999,
		"landing point is centered without changing logical surface position"
	)
	var viewport_size := surface._paint.get_viewport_rect().size
	var front_projection := surface._project_surface_point(
		surface.player_surface_position,
		viewport_size
	)
	var back_projection := surface._project_surface_point(
		-surface.player_surface_position,
		viewport_size
	)
	_check(
		front_projection["visible"] and not back_projection["visible"],
		"2.5D projection hides objects behind spherical horizon"
	)
	var shallow_surface_point := (
		surface.planet_view_rotation.inverse()
		* Vector3(sqrt(0.99), 0.0, 0.1)
	).normalized()
	var shallow_projection := surface._project_surface_point(shallow_surface_point, viewport_size)
	_check(
		float(shallow_projection["scale"]) < 0.6
		and float(shallow_projection["flatten"]) < 0.25,
		"surface objects shrink and flatten near horizon"
	)
	var oblique_surface_point := (
		surface.planet_view_rotation.inverse()
		* Vector3(0.5, 0.0, sqrt(0.75))
	).normalized()
	var ground_projection := surface._project_surface_point(oblique_surface_point, viewport_size)
	var elevated_projection := surface._project_surface_point(
		oblique_surface_point,
		viewport_size,
		700.0
	)
	var planet_center := surface._planet_center(viewport_size)
	_check(
		Vector2(elevated_projection["position"]).distance_to(planet_center)
		> Vector2(ground_projection["position"]).distance_to(planet_center),
		"altitude projects objects farther from globe center"
	)
	var loop_start: Vector3 = surface.player_surface_position
	surface._move_player_on_sphere(Vector2.RIGHT, TAU)
	_check(
		loop_start.dot(surface.player_surface_position) > 0.999999,
		"full spherical circuit returns to the same surface point"
	)
	var initial_globe_rotation: Quaternion = surface.planet_view_rotation
	surface._apply_surface_movement(Vector2.RIGHT, planet.radius * 0.25)
	surface._update_surface_camera_follow(0.5)
	var view_position: Vector3 = surface.planet_view_rotation * surface.player_surface_position
	var projected_screen: Vector2 = surface._project_surface_point(
		surface.player_surface_position,
		viewport_size,
		surface._altitude
	)["position"]
	_check(
		surface._ship_screen_position().distance_to(projected_screen) < 0.001,
		"ship screen position comes directly from spherical projection"
	)
	_check(
		Vector2(view_position.x, view_position.y).length() < 0.0001,
		"surface camera keeps ship exactly centered after movement"
	)
	_check(
		absf(surface.planet_view_rotation.dot(initial_globe_rotation)) < 0.999,
		"grounded camera rotates globe independently under the player"
	)
	_check(
		absf(
			surface._surface_angular_distance(180.0)
			- 180.0 / (planet.radius + SurfaceModeType.HOVER_ALTITUDE)
		) < 0.000001,
		"surface movement uses the real planet radius"
	)
	for i in 20:
		surface._apply_surface_movement(Vector2.RIGHT, planet.radius * 0.08)
		surface._update_surface_camera_follow(0.1)
	view_position = surface.planet_view_rotation * surface.player_surface_position
	_check(
		Vector2(view_position.x, view_position.y).length() < 0.0001,
		"grounded camera continuously keeps player at center"
	)
	_check(
		absf(surface.planet_view_rotation.dot(initial_globe_rotation)) < 0.999,
		"globe rotates independently under the player"
	)
	surface._surface_velocity = surface._screen_direction_to_tangent(Vector2.RIGHT) * 60.0
	surface._update_surface_camera_follow(0.5)
	view_position = surface.planet_view_rotation * surface.player_surface_position
	_check(
		Vector2(view_position.x, view_position.y).length() < 0.0001,
		"surface camera has no directional offset"
	)
	var ship_projection_for_zoom := surface._project_surface_point(
		surface.player_surface_position,
		surface._paint.get_viewport_rect().size,
		surface._altitude
	)
	var ship_scale_before_zoom: Vector2 = surface._ship_visual_scale(ship_projection_for_zoom)
	surface.surface_zoom *= 2.0
	var ship_scale_after_zoom: Vector2 = surface._ship_visual_scale(ship_projection_for_zoom)
	_check(
		absf(ship_scale_after_zoom.x / ship_scale_before_zoom.x - 2.0) < 0.0001,
		"surface ship scales proportionally with zoom"
	)
	surface.surface_zoom = SurfaceModeType.SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT
	var radius_before_zoom: float = surface._planet_radius(surface._paint.get_viewport_rect().size)
	_check(
		is_equal_approx(surface.surface_zoom, SurfaceModeType.SURFACE_ZOOM_DEFAULT),
		"landing starts with one-to-one surface zoom"
	)
	surface.surface_zoom /= SurfaceModeType.SURFACE_ZOOM_STEP
	var radius_after_zoom: float = surface._planet_radius(surface._paint.get_viewport_rect().size)
	_check(radius_after_zoom < radius_before_zoom, "mouse-wheel surface zoom changes rendered radius")
	ship.frame_near(planet)
	var viewport := surface._paint.get_viewport_rect().size
	var one_to_one_radius: float = PlanetType.target_screen_radius(viewport)
	_check(
		absf(ship.camera_zoom * planet.radius - one_to_one_radius) < 0.01,
		"space view frames planet at the shared one-to-one scale"
	)
	surface.begin_grounded(planet)
	_check(
		absf(surface._planet_radius(viewport) - one_to_one_radius) < 0.01,
		"landing preserves the exact one-to-one planet scale"
	)
	_check(SurveyShip.CAMERA_ZOOM_MAX >= 12.0, "space camera supports extreme close zoom")
	var normal_planet_speed := surface._hover_max_speed()
	var normal_planet_angular_speed := normal_planet_speed / (
		planet.radius + SurfaceModeType.HOVER_ALTITUDE
	)
	var original_planet_radius := planet.radius
	planet.radius *= 2.0
	var large_planet_speed := surface._hover_max_speed()
	var large_planet_angular_speed := large_planet_speed / (
		planet.radius + SurfaceModeType.HOVER_ALTITUDE
	)
	planet.radius = original_planet_radius
	_check(
		absf(normal_planet_angular_speed - large_planet_angular_speed) < 0.000001,
		"surface speed keeps the same angular pace on different planet sizes"
	)
	_check(
		large_planet_speed > normal_planet_speed * 1.99,
		"linear surface speed scales with planet radius"
	)
	_check(
		absf(
			normal_planet_angular_speed
			- TAU / SurfaceModeType.SURFACE_CIRCUIT_TIME_AT_MAX_SPEED
		) < 0.000001,
		"maximum surface speed matches configured circuit time"
	)
	var position_before_drift: Vector3 = surface.player_surface_position
	surface._surface_velocity = surface._screen_direction_to_tangent(Vector2.RIGHT) * 60.0
	surface._tick_grounded(0.5)
	_check(
		position_before_drift.dot(surface.player_surface_position) < 0.999999,
		"surface ship keeps moving after thrust is released"
	)
	_check(surface._surface_velocity.length() > 50.0, "surface flight preserves inertia")
	surface.begin_grounded(planet)
	var camera_before_flight: Quaternion = surface.planet_view_rotation
	surface._surface_velocity = (
		surface._screen_direction_to_tangent(Vector2.RIGHT)
		* surface._hover_max_speed()
	)
	for frame in 360:
		surface._tick_surface(1.0 / 60.0)
	var followed_view: Vector3 = surface.planet_view_rotation * surface.player_surface_position
	_check(
		absf(surface.planet_view_rotation.dot(camera_before_flight)) < 0.999,
		"surface camera activates during normal-speed flight"
	)
	_check(
		Vector2(followed_view.x, followed_view.y).length() < 0.001,
		"surface camera keeps ship centered at maximum flight speed"
	)
	surface.begin_grounded(planet)
	var latitude_before: float = surface._current_coordinate().latitude
	surface._apply_surface_movement(Vector2.UP, planet.radius * 0.2)
	_check(surface._current_coordinate().latitude > latitude_before, "north-south movement changes latitude")
	_check(absf(surface.player_surface_position.length() - 1.0) < 0.0001, "surface position remains on the sphere")
	surface._state = SurfaceModeType.SurfaceState.ASCENDING
	surface._altitude = SurfaceModeType.EXIT_ALTITUDE + 1.0
	surface._radial_velocity = 40.0
	surface._surface_velocity = Vector3.ZERO
	surface._finish_launch()
	_check(not ship.surface_mode_active and ship.landed_on == null, "surface launch returns to space view")
	surface.queue_free()
	planet.queue_free()
	ship.queue_free()

##### backup-units-20260915-175616\scripts\universe.gd #####
class_name Universe
extends Node2D

const PREFIX: Array[String] = ["Ke", "Sel", "Vor", "Nyx", "Tal", "Iri", "Myr", "Zha", "Orr", "Lun", "Ash", "Vel"]
const MID: Array[String] = ["ra", "lun", "the", "vos", "kai", "dra", "si", "ae", "or"]
const SUFFIX: Array[String] = ["ne", "os", "ix", "ara", "um", "eth", "is", "or", "yn"]
const KINDS: Array[String] = ["arid", "ocean", "ice", "lava", "gas", "toxic", "carbon"]

var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
var planets: Array[ProcPlanet] = []
var stations: Array[Station] = []
var sun_radius: float = 90.0
var _asteroids: Array[Dictionary] = []


func generate(system_seed: int = 0) -> void:
	if system_seed == 0:
		rng.randomize()
	else:
		rng.seed = system_seed
	sun_radius = rng.randf_range(18000.0, 28000.0)
	_make_sun_body()
	var count := rng.randi_range(6, 8)
	var orbit := rng.randf_range(90000.0, 125000.0)
	for i in count:
		var kind: String = KINDS[i % KINDS.size()]
		if i == 0:
			kind = ["arid", "lava", "carbon"][rng.randi_range(0, 2)]
		elif i == count - 1:
			kind = ["ice", "gas", "carbon"][rng.randi_range(0, 2)]
		elif rng.randf() < 0.35:
			kind = KINDS[rng.randi_range(0, KINDS.size() - 1)]
		var radius := rng.randf_range(5200.0, 12500.0)
		if kind == "gas":
			radius = rng.randf_range(16000.0, 32000.0)
		var planet := ProcPlanet.new()
		planet.z_index = 2
		planet.configure(rng, _make_name(), kind, radius, orbit)
		planet.orbit_speed = OrbitalPhysics.circular_speed(sun_mu(), orbit) / orbit
		if rng.randf() < 0.18:
			planet.orbit_speed *= -1.0
		planet.name = planet.planet_name
		add_child(planet)
		planet.tick(0.0)
		planets.append(planet)
		if kind != "gas" and rng.randf() > 0.55:
			_add_moon(planet)
		elif kind == "gas" and rng.randf() > 0.4:
			_add_moon(planet)
			if rng.randf() > 0.5:
				_add_moon(planet)
		orbit += rng.randf_range(70000.0, 150000.0) + radius * 1.8
	_make_stations()
	_make_asteroids()
	queue_redraw()


func _make_name() -> String:
	return PREFIX[rng.randi_range(0, PREFIX.size() - 1)] + MID[rng.randi_range(0, MID.size() - 1)] + SUFFIX[rng.randi_range(0, SUFFIX.size() - 1)]


func _make_sun_body() -> void:
	var body := StaticBody2D.new()
	body.name = "SunBody"
	var shape := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = sun_radius * 0.9
	shape.shape = circle
	body.add_child(shape)
	add_child(body)


func _add_moon(parent: ProcPlanet) -> void:
	var moon := ProcPlanet.new()
	moon.z_index = 3
	var mk: String = ["ice", "carbon", "arid"][rng.randi_range(0, 2)]
	var r := parent.radius * rng.randf_range(0.12, 0.24)
	var orbit := parent.radius * rng.randf_range(1.9, 3.1)
	moon.configure(rng, parent.planet_name + " " + ["I", "II", "III"][rng.randi_range(0, 2)], mk, r, orbit)
	moon.parent_body = parent
	moon.orbit_speed = OrbitalPhysics.circular_speed(parent.mu, orbit) / orbit
	if rng.randf() < 0.12:
		moon.orbit_speed *= -1.0
	moon.name = moon.planet_name
	add_child(moon)
	planets.append(moon)


func _make_stations() -> void:
	stations.clear()
	var mains: Array[ProcPlanet] = []
	for p in planets:
		if p.parent_body == null:
			mains.append(p)
	if mains.is_empty():
		return
	var first := Station.new()
	first.configure(mains[0], "Stacja", rng)
	add_child(first)
	stations.append(first)
	if mains.size() > 2 and rng.randf() > 0.4:
		var extra := Station.new()
		extra.configure(mains[mini(2, mains.size() - 1)], "Posterunek", rng)
		add_child(extra)
		stations.append(extra)


func _make_asteroids() -> void:
	_asteroids.clear()
	var mains: Array[ProcPlanet] = []
	for p in planets:
		if p.parent_body == null:
			mains.append(p)
	var belt_r := 280000.0
	if mains.size() >= 4:
		belt_r = (mains[2].orbit_radius + mains[3].orbit_radius) * 0.5
	for i in 140:
		var ang := rng.randf() * TAU
		var rad := belt_r + rng.randf_range(-9000.0, 9000.0)
		_asteroids.append({
			"pos": Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * rad,
			"r": rng.randf_range(40.0, 220.0),
			"rot": rng.randf() * TAU,
		})


func nearest(from: Vector2) -> ProcPlanet:
	if planets.is_empty():
		return null
	var best := planets[0]
	var best_d := from.distance_to(best.global_position) - best.radius
	for p in planets:
		var d := from.distance_to(p.global_position) - p.radius
		if d < best_d:
			best = p
			best_d = d
	return best


func gravity_at(world: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	var acc := Vector2.ZERO
	acc += OrbitalPhysics.gravity_at(world, Vector2.ZERO, sun_mu(), sun_radius * 1.05)
	for p in planets:
		acc += p.gravity_at(world)
	return acc


func sun_mu() -> float:
	return OrbitalPhysics.mu_from_surface_gravity(55.0, sun_radius)


func soi_radius(planet: ProcPlanet) -> float:
	var parent_mu := sun_mu()
	var a := planet.orbit_radius
	if planet.parent_body is ProcPlanet:
		parent_mu = (planet.parent_body as ProcPlanet).mu
	var ratio := planet.mu / maxf(parent_mu, 1.0)
	var soi := a * pow(ratio, 0.4) * 1.25
	return maxf(soi, planet.radius * 4.5)


func orbit_status(world: Vector2, vel: Vector2) -> Dictionary:
	var body_name := "sĹ‚oĹ„ce"
	var body_pos := Vector2.ZERO
	var body_vel := Vector2.ZERO
	var body_mu := sun_mu()
	var body_radius := sun_radius
	var focus: ProcPlanet = null
	var best_score := 1.0
	for p in planets:
		var r := world.distance_to(p.global_position)
		var soi := soi_radius(p)
		var score := r / soi
		if score < 1.0 and score < best_score:
			best_score = score
			focus = p
	if focus:
		body_name = focus.planet_name
		body_pos = focus.global_position
		body_vel = focus.inertial_velocity()
		body_mu = focus.mu
		body_radius = focus.radius
	var rel_p := world - body_pos
	var rel_v := vel - body_vel
	var r := maxf(rel_p.length(), 1.0)
	var elements := OrbitalPhysics.elements(rel_p, rel_v, body_mu)
	var bound: bool = elements["bound"]
	var pe: float = elements["pe"]
	var ap: float = elements["ap"]
	var band := _solar_band(world.length())
	return {
		"body": body_name,
